## Hyper-Realistic Cinematic Christmas Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "render_goal": "Cinematic hyper-realistic portrait with cozy Christmas vibes",
  "subject": {
    "gender": "female",
    "age_range": "mid-20s",
    "pose": "sitting by a fireplace holding a cup of hot cocoa",
    "expression": "warm, joyful smile",
    "hair": "long, slightly wavy",
    "skin_detail": "glowing, natural texture"
  },
  "wardrobe": {
    "outfit": "red cozy sweater dress with fluffy socks"
  },
  "environment": {
    "location": "living room decorated for Christmas",
    "background": {
      "fireplace": "lit with warm flames",
      "decor": "Christmas tree, lights, garlands, presents",
      "atmosphere": "soft warm glow, cinematic haze"
    }
  },
  "lighting_and_color": {
    "lighting_style": "warm cinematic",
    "color_palette": "reds, golds, soft whites"
  }
}
```

## Low-Angle Paragliding Selfie Action Shot

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "subject_reference": {
      "Gender": "Female", A Beautiful Woman in her 20s.
      "facial_preservation": "High fidelity, exact features, no stylization, no alteration to identity"
    },
    "prompts": {
      "positive_prompt": "High-resolution aerial action photography of [Reference Woman] paragliding. Low-angle wide-angle selfie shot captured via a selfie stick held in her left hand. She is suspended in a professional paragliding harness with legs freely dangling, wearing a black bikini and simple flip-flops. Her right hand grips the paragliding control handle. The background is an expansive, vibrant tropical coastline with deep turquoise ocean and clear blue sky. Bright natural daylight, sharp focus, hyper-realistic skin texture, cinematic composition, dynamic motion, true-to-life proportions.",
      "negative_prompt": "CG, 3d render, anime, cartoon, painting, drawing, illustration, altered face, distorted features, bad anatomy, deformed hands, missing fingers, helmet, sunglasses, wetsuit, sneakers, cloudy, dark lighting, blurry, noise, grain, low resolution"
    },
    "scene_details": {
      "camera_angle": "Low perspective, wide-angle, looking up from selfie stick",
      "lighting": "Bright natural daylight, hard sunlight",
      "attire": [
        "Black bikini",
        "Simple flip-flops"
      ],
      "pose_constraints": {
        "left_hand": "Holding selfie stick pointed toward self",
        "right_hand": "Holding paragliding control handle",
        "legs": "Dangling freely"
      }
    },
    "technical_settings": {
      "aspect_ratio": "9:16",
      "style": "Ultra-realistic, raw photography"
    }
  }
}
```

## 2D/3D Mixed-Dimension Illusion Room

> Elle çizilmiş manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cozy room where the wall looks like a giant paper illustration, flat ink lines and watercolor washes, yet a corner of the “paper wall” is peeled back, revealing a fully 3D forest behind it. The peeled edge curls outward like thick cardstock, casting a real shadow on the illustrated surface. The illusion: the 2D drawn objects (a drawn lamp, drawn shelf) cast realistic 3D shadows, while the 3D forest casts faint “ink” shadows as if it’s becoming a drawing. A cat sits half-in, half-out: front paws in 3D forest moss, back body in 2D sketch form, seamlessly blended. Soft morning light, gentle pastel palette, tactile paper fibers visible, high detail, whimsical but believable “mixed-dimension” realism.
```

## High-Contrast Portrait with Direct Flash and Faux Fur

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
8k raw photo with natural ISO grain, sharp micro-contrast, tactile surface details. Keep the reference face and body proportions fully consistent. Upper torso framed, cut at mid-chest. Subject's head tilted 5 degrees right, eyes gazing playfully 10 degrees right and 5 degrees up, pupils slightly dilated. Lips parted, tongue peeking out in a mischievous gesture. Skin displays visible skin porosity, sharp micro-details, and fine tactile skin grain, with a subtle dusting of freckles just below the eyes. Natural vellus hair is visible. High-voltage specular highlights and flash feedback on skin, forehead, and nose, indicative of direct flash. Subject's left arm raised, fingers tensed gripping a Silver Mist iPhone with three camera lenses and an active flash module, emitting a bright, circular cool white light. Voluminous, shaggy {argument name="fur color" default="Snow White"} faux fur hood with curly fibers frames the face, lined with smooth Pale Rose satin on the inner ears and trim. A matching Snow White faux fur coat/shawl with fluffy drape covers the shoulders. Direct on-camera flash creates high-contrast illumination, hard shadows behind the phone and subject, and intense specular highlights on all surfaces. Deep depth of field, f/16 aperture, hyperfocal distance, ensuring the background is fully in sharp focus with zero bokeh. Background features a smooth Light Greige wall. A partial Obsidian Black rectangular frame is visible on the left, reflecting an indistinct dark area. Projecting a playful, mischievous aura.
```

## Nano Banana Pro X Header Image Generation Prompt

> Minimal, retro logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
# ===== USER CONTROLS =====
title_text: "{argument name="display string" default="<<YOUR_TEXT>>"}"
title_style_preset: "{argument name="logo design preset" default="cool"}"  # cool, cute, cool_cute, luxury, pop, cyber, minimal, retro, kawaii
face_zoom: "{argument name="zoom on/off" default="off"}"            # off, on

subject: >
  Create a 3:1 wide banner using the provided reference image content.
  The primary face must be centered inside an invisible safe zone (not drawn),
  and a title logo (derived from title_text + title_style_preset) must be placed inside the same safe zone.

composition: >
  Aspect ratio 3:1.
  Invisible safe zone (DO NOT draw it):
  - Left margin: 15% of width
  - Right margin: 15% of width
  - Top margin: 16% of height
  - Bottom margin: 16% of height
  Keep the entire face AND the entire title logo fully inside this safe zone.
  Background may extend to the edges.

action: >
  Place the reference image content with maximum fidelity.
  Auto-crop/scale/reposition to fit a 3:1 banner with no stretching.
  Detect the primary face (if multiple faces, pick the largest/most prominent).
  Center the primary face within the safe zone both horizontally and vertically.
  Never crop off the face/eyes; keep the full face comfortably inside the safe zone (not near edges).

  Face zoom behavior:
  - If face_zoom is OFF: keep the original framing as much as possible while still centering the face in the safe zone.
  - If face_zoom is ON: zoom/crop to a bust-up portrait centered on the face
    (head and shoulders visible; include upper chest; do not crop the top of the head or chin; keep facial features prominent).

  Create negative space inside the safe zone for the title logo by adjusting crop/reposition/out
```

## Epic Figurative Art Prompt for 'Kanji of the Year'

> Manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
GENERAL STYLE & MOOD:
“Epic Figurative Art.” The prompt requires multi-layered analysis of the user’s provided home screen (articles, headings, used apps, notifications, colors) to condense all “elements” of the year into a single still image. It should be a fusion of philosophical, profound realism and surreal design.

TASK & ANALYSIS:
1. Comprehensively scan the text information within the provided home screen to extract frequently used vocabulary, interests, emotional fluctuations, and fragments of daily life.
2. Integrate these “total elements of the year” and select one ultimate “single Kanji character” that symbolizes the user’s journey.
3. Construct the “strokes” of that Kanji character as a three-dimensional collection of concrete events analyzed (e.g., work tools, hobby fragments, motifs from read articles).

SUBJECT:
The “single Kanji character” embodying the user’s year, centrally positioned.
- This character is a “collection of micro-stories.” Upon closer inspection, the parts of the character should be formed by specific objects that colored the user’s year (e.g., designs symbolizing specific news articles, daily tools, fragments of memory).
- Maintain perfect readability of the Kanji and ensure it has a strong skeletal structure.

ENVIRONMENT & BACKGROUND (The Total Year Narrative):
The background should also be depicted not merely as scenery, but as an epic panorama covering “all elements of the year.”
- Background composition: Behind the character, the analyzed events of the year should spread out as scenery, either chronologically or thematically (e.g., symbols of challenged projects, the atmosphere of visited places, images of engrossing news).
- Consistency: The central Kanji should be the core of the “world” of that year, with all background elements converging towards the character or overflowing from it.

LIGHTING & TEXTURE:
- Profound texture that conveys “layers of memory.” Metal, organic matter, light particles, and digital data should mix, possessing physically correct reflections and shadows.
- Background light sources (dramatic backlighting or sidelight) should illuminate the character, highlighting the multilayered elements of the year through shadow.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Accurately depict the Kanji shape (hooks, sweeps, stroke count) so it is correctly legible in Japanese.
- Arrange “elements of the year” in both the background and the character, ensuring the overall screen is not cluttered and maintains harmony as a single work of art.
- 4K high resolution. Completely exclude meaningless symbols, garbled text, or broken forms.

Input Information
[[ {argument name="input information" default="URL of your home screen/article here"} ]]
```

## Wintry Cinematic Selfie Prompt (Identity Locked)

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an ultra-realistic cinematic photograph in a film-still aesthetic, based strictly on the face from the provided reference photo, preserving 100% facial likeness with absolutely no changes to facial structure or hair.
The image is captured as a low-angle selfie, with the camera slightly tilted upward, creating a dynamic perspective that emphasizes the grandeur of a wooden mountain chalet in the background, its roof heavily covered in snow. The upper lines of the building recede into perspective, enhancing the dramatic low-angle composition.
The woman is framed in a tight close-up. Her face is subtly tilted forward toward the camera, making her facial features appear more expressive: full lips, a clearly defined jawline, and balanced proportions. Her expression is calm and confident, with a soft, sensual half-smile.
She is wearing a voluminous, natural fluffy fur coat that fills the lower part of the frame due to the shooting angle. In her hand—raised slightly higher than usual because of the selfie perspective—she holds a large glass of red wine close to the camera, creating an almost three-dimensional effect. The wine has a deep ruby tone with cinematic softness, and gold rings are visible on her fingers.
Her straight hair is lightly dusted with snowflakes. Above her head, the sky and the chalet roof are visible, both densely covered in snow. Large snowflakes drift slowly through the air, appearing in both the foreground and background, adding depth and atmosphere.
Film look characteristics:
soft film grain;
pastel, slightly muted color palette;
gentle highlights on the glass and hair;
warm skin tones contrasting with cool bluish-gray hues of the snow and sky;
subtle vignette around the corners;
35mm film effect: smooth light transitions, natural shadows, no digital sharpness;
fine dust and microscopic film imperfections (optional).
The overall atmosphere is aesthetic, luxurious, relaxed, and wintry, evoking the feeling of a living cinematic moment and true presence in the scene.
```

## Ultra-Realistic Cinematic Street Photograph Prompt

```
no realism, no photorealistic texture, pure anime hand-drawn look.
```

```
  - Right margin: 15% of width
  - Top margin: 16% of height
  - Bottom margin: 16% of height

action: >
  Place the reference image content with maximum fidelity.

  Detect the primary face (if multiple faces, pick the largest/most prominent).
  Center the primary face within the safe zone both horizontally and vertically.
  Never crop off the face/eyes; keep the full face comfortably inside the safe zone (not near edges).

  Face zoom behavior:
  - If face_zoom is OFF: keep the original framing as much as possible while still centering the face in the safe zone.
  - If face_zoom is ON: zoom/crop to a bust-up portrait centered on the face
    (head and shoulders visible; include upper chest; do no
```

## Christmas Surprise Box Dynamic Action Shot

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Dynamic action photography, explosion composition, festive art style). An explosive surprise composition where a young woman bursts out from a giant Christmas gift box in a moment of pure joy.

The Action: The character is wearing a white faux fur winter coat and a red Santa hat, leaping upward with arms raised in celebration, frozen in mid-air jump. A huge red and gold wrapped gift box below her is exploding open with the lid flying off.

Dynamic Elements: Colorful ribbons and wrapping paper pieces explode outward in all directions. Snow and magical glitter burst upward like confetti. Small snow-covered pine trees with colorful Christmas lights shoot out from the box. Gift tags, ornaments, and tinsel swirl in the air. Snowflakes scatter in the explosive motion.

Background: Soft-focused cozy living room with Christmas decorations, fireplace, creating warm festive atmosphere.

Lighting: Magical glow emanating from inside the gift box, illuminating the character from below with warm golden light. Colorful Christmas lights create bokeh trails. Dynamic motion blur on flying objects.

Technical: High-speed photography freeze frame, particle explosion effects, dynamic composition, vibrant festive colors, 8k resolution, joyful and energetic aesthetic.
```

## Surreal Character Trapped in a Giant Christmas Snow Globe

> 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Surrealism, fantasy art, macro photography style). A magical composition where the specific character is captured inside a giant, life-sized glass snow globe. [CRITICAL: Keep the face identical to the source image, maintain consistent facial features within the glass distortion].

Inside the Globe: The character is wearing a {argument name="clothing inside globe" default="white faux fur winter coat and a red Santa hat"}, catching falling snowflakes with her hands. The environment inside includes miniature snow-covered pine trees decorated with colorful Christmas lights twinkling in red, green, blue, and gold. A tiny wooden cabin sits among the trees. Swirling magical glitter and snow dust fill the air inside the sphere.

Outside the Globe: The background is a blurry, cozy living room with a fireplace, emphasizing that the character is inside the ornament.

Lighting: The snow globe is glowing from within, illuminated by the warm colorful glow of the Christmas tree lights mixing with cool magical blue and white light on the falling snow. The character's face is beautifully lit by this magical mixed lighting. External warm light reflects off the curved glass surface, creating rainbow prismatic effects.

Technical: Ray tracing reflections on the glass, refraction effects, crystal clear focus on the character, magical atmosphere, ethereal and dreamy aesthetic, 8k resolution, intricate details of the snowflakes and Christmas lights bokeh.
```

## Modern Loft Staircase Portrait Identity Preservation Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use ONLY the woman from my reference photo. Keep her face, identity, facial structure, body proportions, anatomy and pose EXACTLY the same. No beautification, no stylization, no age or body change.

She is sitting on an indoor staircase, leaning slightly back, legs bent, relaxed posture, a MacBook-style laptop on her lap (no visible logo). Natural soft smile, same head angle and body alignment as the reference.

Outfit: cream oversized knit sweater with visible fabric texture, dark charcoal straight-fit jeans, brown leather sneakers with white soles, minimal gold bracelet, thin ring, elegant wristwatch, small leather handbag placed beside her.

Environment: modern loft-style interior, raw concrete staircase with natural texture, smooth matte concrete or warm wood panel wall, large side window, clean architectural lines, no perforated metal walls, no identical stock background.

Lighting: soft natural daylight from the side, slightly warm tone, realistic shadows, skin tones preserved, no dramatic contrast.

Camera & quality: ultra-photorealistic, 8K resolution, maximum detail, professional photography, 35mm lens, f/2.8 aperture, natural depth of field, sharp focus, cinematic realism, aspect ratio 3:2.

Negative prompt: different woman, face change, identity change, same red sweater, yellow beret, perforated wall, identical staircase, stock photo look, copy, duplicate scene, watermark, logo, text, illustration, cartoon, painting, oversharpening, blur, noise.
```

Long dark hair, slightly tousled, with natural glam makeup and a calm, confident expression looking directly at the camera.
```
Elevator interior features brushed steel walls with soft reflections and minimal background detail. Lighting is direct camera flash with controlled highlights, creating glossy skin texture and sharp reflections on the sequins. ## 2x2 Grid Character Consistency Prompt (JSON)
```

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "layout": {
    "type": "2x2 grid",

  },
  "source": {
    "mode": "image_reference",
    "reference_images": {
      "primary": "base_person_image"
    },
    "preserve": {
      "same_person": true,
      "identity_lock": true,
      "keep_face": true,
      "keep_hairstyle": true,
      "keep_outfit": true

    "variation_rule": "each frame uses a different composition and angle",
    "base_shot_type": "middle shot",
    "frame_variations": [
      "sitting at a small apartment table, shoulders slightly slumped, holding her stomach with one hand, faint amused smile",
      "leaning back in a chair, head tilted up, eyes half-closed, one hand resting on her stomach as if enduring hunger",
      "standing in the kitchen area, arms loosely crossed over her midsection, looking toward the fridge with a resigned playful expression",
      "sitting on the sofa, knees drawn slightly up, one hand pressing lightly on her stomach, soft laugh at her own situation"
    ]
  },
  "accessories": {
    "handheld_item": "empty plate or nothing in hands"
  },
  "environment": {
    "location": "small apartment interior",
    "room_type": "kitchen and living area",
    "time": "late afternoon",
    "context": "missed lunch, waiting until dinner",
    "details": [
      "simple dining table",
      "compact kitchen",
      "sofa and low table",
      "everyday lived-in apartment feel"
    ]
  },
  "lighting": {
    "primary": "soft natural daylight from window",
    "secondary": "subtle indoor ambient light"
  },
  "camera": {
    "shot_type": "middle shot",
    "lens_feel": "slight wide-angle",
    "framing": "waist-up, subject centered with gentle variation per frame"
  },
  "color_tone": "natural color, neutral skin tones, realistic indoor daylight balance",
  "image_quality": {
    "realism": "photorealistic",
    "style": "casual everyday snapshot"
  }
}
```

```
A medium close-up photograph of a plus-size woman with dark hair tied up in a messy bun, sitting on a weathered concrete rooftop ledge during the golden hour sunset. She is wearing a light pink halter-neck sports bra with a floral patterned underband and matching leggings covered in a vibrant, multi-colored floral print. Large silver statement earrings dangle from her ears, and she wears strappy sandals on her feet. Her body is angled slightly away, and her head is turned in profile, looking towards the left. The warm, soft light of the setting sun backlights her figure, creating a warm glow. The background is a blurred cityscape with warm hues. The camera is positioned close to the subject. The signature 'Willy' is in the bottom-right corner.—ar 3:4
```

```
},
```

## Photorealistic Portrait with Shallow Depth of Field

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A photorealistic portrait of a young Asian woman with voluminous, long, wavy black hair and fair skin. She is wearing thin-rimmed, round glasses and has a playful expression, winking one eye with a subtle smile. Outfit: She is dressed in a black, long-sleeved button-down shirt dress with thin white vertical pinstripes. She is wearing sheer black thigh-high stockings. Pose: She is seated in a curved, cream-colored leather armchair. Her body is angled slightly forward. Her right hand is raised, with long manicured fingers delicately touching her cheek and jawline in a playful, posing gesture. Environment: The background features soft, floor-length beige or off-white sheer curtains, suggesting an indoor hotel room or bedroom setting at night. Lighting & Style: Warm, soft indoor lighting that creates gentle highlights on her hair and the silky texture of the shirt. High-resolution photography, sharp focus on the face, slightly candid but polished aesthetic. Camera Angle: Slightly high angle (tilted down about 10–15 degrees), seated eye-level. Focal Length: 35mm or 50mm. (This creates a natural perspective without too much distortion, typical of high-end smartphone portraits or standard portrait lenses). Aperture: f/2.0 to f/2.8. (This provides a shallow depth of field, keeping her face and hand in sharp focus while softly blurring the texture of the curtains in the background). Shutter Speed: 1/60s or 1/100s. (Standard for indoor handheld shots to avoid motion blur). ISO: 400 to 800. (Slightly higher sensitivity to account for the warm, dim indoor lighting without introducing too much grain). Lighting Setup: Soft key light from the front-top (likely a ceiling fixture or ring light), creating the specular highlights on the nose and forehead.
```

```
"A hyper-realistic, extreme close-up beauty portrait of a stunning woman. She is raising her arm to cover the left side of her face, wearing a vibrant red ribbed-knit sweater. Only one mesmerizing hazel-green eye is visible, featuring dramatic black winged eyeliner, gold glitter eyeshadow, and long lashes. Her lips are full and painted with a matte red lipstick that matches the sweater perfectly. Flawless airbrushed skin, soft studio lighting, high fashion editorial style, sharp focus on the eye no facial change same as it is on the reference picture."

Prompt 2:
"Create a highly photorealistic, cinematic travel portrait of a young woman whose facial identity exactly matches the uploaded reference image, with 100% preservation of facial structure, proportions, skin texture, eye shape, lips, nose, brows, moles, and natural expression. She has a soft, relaxed smile with calm eyes and is gently looking toward the camera. Her pose shows her leaning out of a train window, with both arms resting comfortably on the window frame and her upper body slightly leaned forward in a relaxed, cozy posture.
She has dark brown straight hair worn loose and slightly tucked under a white knitted winter beanie with a soft knit texture. Her makeup is natural winter-style, featuring an even fair skin tone, light blush, soft eyeliner, natural lashes, and subtle pink lips. She is wearing a cream or off-white cozy winter sweater made of soft, warm knit fabric suitable for cold weather.
The scene is set inside a red panoramic train traveling through snowy alpine mountains. The background includes snow-covered pine trees, mountain slopes blanketed in snow, curving red train cars visible behind her, and thick snow piled along the tracks, clearly conveying a winter landscape. Lighting is natural cold daylight with soft diffusion, cool winter tones, gentle highlights on the face, and natural shadows.
The overall style is a cinematic travel portrait with a DSLR photography look, moderate depth of field that keeps the subject sharp while preserving scenic background detail, and a peaceful, cozy, adventurous winter travel mood. Use high-resolution rendering with realistic skin texture, high detail, and cool winter color grading with soft contrast, maintaining strict identity lock to the reference image throughout."

Prompt 3:
"Create A hyper-realistic person (Retro vintage, grainy but bright aesthetic. A girl(face exactly same as the reference image) wearing a perfect red wine colored red frock, holding a A bunch of 10-12 flowers of roses in her hands. The style feels like a 90s movie, romantic, wind blowing girl. She is standing against a solid wall with deep shadows and dramatic contrast, mysterious and artistic atmosphere."
```

## Cinematic Winter Snowfall Portrait

```
Cinematic full-body walking shot of this uploaded girl , walking calmly along the roadside of a quiet small American town during winter snowfall. She wears a red winter parka with a fur-trimmed hood, dark gray knit scarf, gray beanie, black slim pants, and brown fur-lined winter boots — outfit matching the reference image exactly. A small crossbody bag hangs naturally at her side.

Fresh snow lightly covers the sidewalk and fallen leaves, with small-town houses, bare trees, and street lamps softly blurred in the background. ARRI Alexa parallax camera, gentle forward motion, strong foreground–background depth separation. Cold winter atmosphere, soft overcast natural light, muted winter color palette (cool whites, soft grays, deep reds, subtle browns). Cinematic realism, ultra-detailed textures, shallow depth of field, soft film grain, calm and introspective mood.
```

## Flash Photography Birthday Portrait Prompt

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high-resolution 3:4 birthday portrait of the person in the input image, keeping their exact face, identity, and features. Shot indoors at night with a strong camera flash that creates bright highlights, vivid reflections, and a lively party mood. The subject reclines on a white bed, turning toward the camera with a soft, confident smile, wearing a pastel pink off-shoulder lace dress with sheer long sleeves and floral texture. Rose-gold ‘HAPPY BIRTHDAY’ foil balloons on the wall reflect the flash intensely; some letters fall outside the frame. Pink balloons on the bed glow with specular reflections. Flash lighting makes the skin luminous and glossy, with sharp catchlights, defined contours, warm blush, glossy lips, and subtle shadows. The overall color style is vibrant, saturated, and Instagram-ready, with a fun, glamorous nightlife vibe.
```

## Polished Indoor Portrait with Shallow Depth of Field

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Subject: A photorealistic portrait of a young Asian woman with voluminous, long, wavy black hair and fair skin. She is wearing thin-rimmed, round glasses and has a playful expression, winking one eye with a subtle smile.
Outfit: She is dressed in a black, long-sleeved button-down shirt dress with thin white vertical pinstripes. She is wearing sheer black thigh-high stockings.
Pose: She is seated in a curved, cream-colored leather armchair. Her body is angled slightly forward. Her right hand is raised, with long manicured fingers delicately touching her cheek and jawline in a playful, posing gesture.
Environment: The background features soft, floor-length beige or off-white sheer curtains, suggesting an indoor hotel room or bedroom setting at night.
Lighting & Style: Warm, soft indoor lighting that creates gentle highlights on her hair and the silky texture of the shirt. High-resolution photography, sharp focus on the face, slightly candid but polished aesthetic.
Camera Angle: Slightly high angle (tilted down about 10–15 degrees), seated eye-level.
Focal Length: 35mm or 50mm. (This creates a natural perspective without too much distortion, typical of high-end smartphone portraits or standard portrait lenses).
Aperture: f/2.0 to f/2.8. (This provides a shallow depth of field, keeping her face and hand in sharp focus while softly blurring the texture of the curtains in the background).
Shutter Speed: 1/60s or 1/100s. (Standard for indoor handheld shots to avoid motion blur).
ISO: 400 to 800. (Slightly higher sensitivity to account for the warm, dim indoor lighting without introducing too much grain).
Lighting Setup: Soft key light from the front-top (likely a ceiling fixture or ring light), creating the specular highlights on the nose and forehead.
```

## Image to Fragmented Stone Sculpture Style Transfer

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "task": "image_to_image_style_transfer",
  "input_image": "{{USER_IMAGE}}",
  "prompt": "Create a hyper-real 3D studio composition that recreates the main subject from the provided image as a fragmented stone assemblage. The subject must be built from separate, clearly detached rock pieces with small visible gaps between shards (no pieces merging). Material look: fragmented slate + sandstone shards with chiseled edges, crisp fractures, visible stone grain, micro-scratches, and realistic roughness. Color palette: predominantly dark slate with subtle warm-ochre sandstone accents. Lighting: soft studio key light from top-left, gentle fill, subtle contact shadows under each shard, realistic ambient occlusion in crevices, clean reflections kept minimal. Background: minimal off-white seamless backdrop, no texture. Framing: centered, clean, straight-on, subject fully readable. Add a few tiny debris chips floating or resting near the base for depth. Preserve the subject’s identity, proportions, and recognizable silhouette from the input image while transforming all surfaces into stone fragments. Hyper-real, high detail, sharp focus, 8k render quality.",
  "negative_prompt": "text, typography, logo, watermark, signature, extra props, busy background, fog, heavy bloom, cartoon, illustration, lowpoly, plastic, metal, glossy paint, melted shapes, merged fragments, unreadable subject, blur, noise, low resolution, oversharpening halos, distorted face/body, extra limbs, deformed geometry",
  "output": {
    "aspect_ratio": "use_input_aspect_ratio",
    "background": "off_white",
    "camera": {
      "angle": "straight_on",
      "framing": "centered",
      "distance": "medium"
    }
  },
  "params": {
    "style_strength": 0.75,
    "identity_preservation": 0.9,
    "detail_level": "very_high",
    "lighting_preset": "soft_studio_top_left",
    "shadow_intensity": "subtle",
    "gap_visibility": "clear",
    "debris_chips": "few_tiny",
    "no_text": true
  }
}
```

```
A candid photo of a young Asian woman sitting comfortably on a light-colored sofa indoors.
```

## Four Seasons Panorama of Asakusa and Skytree

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic digital illustration: A view of the futuristic Skytree across the Sumida River from the historic Asakusa waterfront. Depict this scene seamlessly as a continuous composition showing the cycle of the four seasons.
The composition progresses naturally from left to right, flowing through Winter, Spring, Summer, and Autumn.
Left side (Winter): Cold snowy landscape, snow-covered historic buildings (Bund buildings), elements of ice and cloudy sky.
Center-left (Spring): Snow melting, fresh green sprouts, bright flowers like cherry blossoms and rapeseed flowers (image of Sumida River in Spring) in full bloom.
Center-right (Summer): Lush green vegetation, bright sunlight, strong reflections on the river surface, blue sky, and a vibrant summer scene.
Right side (Autumn): Vivid autumn colors of gold, orange, and red foliage, a late autumn scene with a harvest mood.
There are no visible boundaries between the seasons; the weather, lighting, and vegetation smoothly merge to form a unified and harmonious panorama. Express the rich details symbolizing the flow of time, cinematic lighting, 8K resolution, and extremely detailed textures.
```

## Image-to-Snowman Conversion Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Minimal, elle çizilmiş illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
# Describe the number, name, and appearance characteristics of the characters in the reference image here
[{argument name="Character Appearance" default="A girl wearing a cloak and a girl in a sailor uniform"}]

A watercolor illustration where all the above characters have transformed into cute “snowmen.”

Conversion Rules:
- The bodies of all characters will take the shape of a “snowman without limbs,” consisting of stacked white snowballs.
- The whole body is composed of 2 or 3 snowballs. Taller characters should have 3 segments.
- Eliminate human skin, limbs, and clothing details, making the body out of snow.
- To identify each character, only their characteristic “hats,” “ribbons,” and “image-colored buttons or scarves” should be attached to the snowman.
- The face should use simple snowman parts (black round eyes, orange or round nose).

★ Important Material Rule (Hair Correction):
- [Highest Priority] Hair parts must never be drawn as “human hair” or “wigs.”
- The shape of the hair is maintained, but the material is entirely “pure white snow mass.”
- Completely ignore the original character's hair color (such as black or brown) and express it in the snow's original “solid white (#FFFFFF).”
- Do not draw individual strands of hair; depict it as a “single mass” of compacted snow.
- Eliminate the boundary between the head snowball and the hairstyle part, integrating them like a single large snow sculpture.
- The texture should not be smooth but should have the “roughness” and “unevenness” characteristic of snow.
- The hair shape should be drawn as a “white silhouette” integrated with the snowman's head. The ends of the hair should not be sharp but rounded and plump, like melting snow.

Art Style:
- Analog-feeling watercolor painting.
- White drawing paper texture.
- Outlines are soft, and colors are pale and blurred.
- The background is white or a faint snowy landscape.
```

## Chrono-Holiday Artisan: Christmas Card Re-render by Era

> Moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
ROLE: You are The Chrono-Holiday Artisan, a designer who exists outside of time, curating Christmas cards from every era of human history (1850–2060).

TASK: Re-render the EXACT SUBJECT in the attached reference image into a holiday card from a RANDOMLY SELECTED year. 

STRICT GUIDELINES:
1. THE ERA: Pick one random year (e.g., {argument name="example year 1" default="1894"}, {argument name="example year 2" default="1956"}, {argument name="example year 3" default="1982"}, {argument name="example year 4" default="2045"}) and strictly apply that era's specific printing technology, film grain, and color palette.
2. THE TEXT: Display ONLY a generic, period-accurate greeting (e.g., "Season's Greetings", "Merry Christmas", "Happy Holidays"). 
3. THE EXCLUSION: ABSOLUTELY NO names, NO "From the family", NO signatures.
4. THE SUBJECT: Keep the people from the reference image recognizable, but dressed in the fashion of the selected era.

--ar 3:4 --iw 2 --stylize 400
```

## Cinematic London Morning Restaurant Portrait

```
Create a photorealistic image of a young woman sitting at a relaxed outdoor restaurant in London on a cool Wednesday morning in March 2025. The sky is clear, the spring air is crisp, and the city feels calm as it wakes up. She is the focal point of the shot—wearing a light scarf, gently stirring her tea while gazing off to the side with a thoughtful expression. Everything behind her, from the waiter moving about to the soft morning traffic, should fall into a smooth blur, giving the picture the effortless, candid atmosphere of a moment casually snapped on a phone.
```

## 90s Yearbook Grid Collage

> Retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A 3x3 grid collage of 9 distinct, awkwardly posed vintage 1990s American yearbook portraits.

**Layout & Subjects:**

* **Center Panel (Row 2, Column 2):** Contains the main subject: [use the person in the attached photo as a late teenager], with a deer-in-headlights look wearing a very loud, geometrically patterned "ugly" knit sweater in clashing colors (purple, green, orange, blue) layered over a white collared shirt.

* **Surrounding 8 Panels:** Fill with diverse classmates featuring comically awkward expressions and exaggerated 90s styles:
    * **Top Row:** A girl with a massive, frizzy perm and thick glasses grinning goofily; a guy with a huge, feathered mullet in a denim jacket looking uncomfortable; a girl with a severe bowl cut and a strained, toothy smile.
    * **Middle Row (Left/Right):** A guy with a tall flat-top haircut giving a cheesy thumbs-up; another guy with a severe center part and a confused, open-mouthed stare.
      * **Bottom Row:** A girl with wildly crimped hair and a pained grimace; a guy wearing large, thick-rimmed tortoise-shell glasses, textured pastel knit crewneck sweater layered over a white collared shirt has a polite, slightly awkward smile; another girl with huge, teased bangs and a forced, tight-lipped grin.
**Style & Tech:**
Classic "Olan Mills" studio aesthetic applied uniformly to all 9 panels. All backgrounds are the generic mottled blue hand-painted canvas. Lighting is flat, broad studio flash with subtle rim lighting. Image quality features soft focus, heavy film grain, warm Kodak Gold 200 color grading, and a soft vignette over each individual panel.
```

```
A hyper-realistic medium portrait of a stunning {argument name="age" default="20-year-old"} woman sitting at a table in a cozy, dimly lit upscale restaurant, posing directly for the camera. She has shoulder-length wavy {argument name="hair color" default="chestnut"} hair and expressive {argument name="eye color" default="hazel"} eyes that are making direct eye contact with the lens. She is wearing a chic, textured {argument name="sweater color" default="cream-colored"} cashmere sweater and a delicate gold necklace.
She is leaning slightly forward, resting her chin gently on her hand, with a half-empty glass of white wine and a small flickering candle on the rustic wooden table in front of her. The lighting is cinematic and warm, utilizing the candlelight to create soft shadows on her face (Rembrandt lighting), highlighting natural skin texture, visible pores, and slight imperfections to ensure realism.
The background is a beautiful, creamy bokeh of the restaurant interior, featuring blurred warm hanging lights, brick walls, and faint silhouettes of other diners. Shot on a Sony A7R IV with an 85mm f/1.2 GM lens, depth of field, 8k resolution, raw photography style, highly detailed, photorealistic. --ar 1:1
```

A centered, high-contrast digital photograph capturing THE_SUBJECT poised, in profile view on a subway platform, looking sharply to the left with a neutral expression and heavy winged eyeliner. THE_SUBJECT'S hair in a messy updo secured by hair clip shaped like a bunch of bananas (as shown in the second reference image). She wears a {argument name="top color" default="pink"} spaghetti-strap camisole, layered over visible lace lingerie, paired with white lace shorts, assorted necklaces and silver bracelets. She is adjusting the hem of her top with her fingertips. A light green crocodile-texture shoulder bag hangs from her shoulder. The subject is in sharp focus, embodying Y2K urban street style, while immediately behind her, a silver subway train rushes horizontally past with dramatic, linear motion blur, streaking the metallic texture and windows to emphasize the chaotic urban speed contrasted by her fashionable stillness. The scene is lit by ambient artificial subway lighting, featuring a warm yellow-green fluorescent overhead diffusion cast and soft, diffused shadows, captured at eye-level with a 35mm or 50mm feel and an aperture of f/4.0 to f/5.6, focusing on the texture of the lace and the dynamic background motion.

```

Her hair is long to mid-back length with soft, wispy bangs, slightly flowing in the breeze.
```

## Y2K Flash Portrait at Christmas Cafe

> Fotogerçekçi, retro moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a hyper-realistic portrait of a cute Asian woman. She is sitting with her chin resting on her hand at a dark wooden table in front of a vintage-style café.

Fashion & Outfit:
She wears a shiny black leather jacket, slightly open to reveal a white inner top, showing a beautiful neckline. Matched with a fitted black mini-skirt set. She also wears black fishnet stockings. Add a fluffy fur hat over her outfit.

Background:
In front of a marble-grey café exterior, decorated with a Christmas theme. Include a realistic Christmas tree, pine decorations, and festive ornaments.

Lighting & Tone:
Warm orange tungsten lighting for an inviting, cozy atmosphere, combined with a strong direct flash hitting the subject straight on, making her skin bright and glowing, contrasting with the slightly darker background.
The photo should feel Y2K digital compact-camera style—sharp 8K resolution, realistic skin texture, with light film grain.

Hair & Makeup:
Long dark-brown hair blowing slightly in the wind, messy layered strands with a few pieces softly covering part of her face—stylish, cool, and subtly sexy.
Her skin is very fair.
Makeup style: Douyin/Korean—long curled lashes, soft flushed pink blush on cheeks and nose tip, glossy pink lips.
She has Christmas-themed nail art.

Props:
On the table:
• A large bouquet wrapped in brown kraft paper, containing {argument name="flower 1" default="cotton flowers"} and {argument name="flower 2" default="pine leaves"}.
• A gold {argument name="camera model" default="Canon IXY 30S"} camera placed on the table.

She holds a Christmas-decorated drink in her right hand and smiles slightly.

Additional Visual Style:
The flash creates extremely bright, slightly yellow-tinted skin with a hard shadow behind her.
High-contrast, ultra-sharp 8K image.
Warm ambient sunlight.
{argument name="film style" default="Fuji Film Pro 400H"} color style with old-lens filter and light film grain.
The subject should appear bright and stand out clearly from the background.
```

## Character 'Participation Screen' Reveal Prompt

> Minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
layering:
  order:
    - text_layer
    - character_layer
    - background_layer
  strict: true

subject:
  description: >
    Preserve the subject exactly as in the reference image, keeping all facial features,
    colors, hairstyle, clothing, and proportions. No redesign or reinterpretation.
  framing: >
    Bust-up composition (head to upper torso), making the subject appear large and prominent.
  integrity: full_identity_preservation
  detail_level: ultra_high_resolution
  micro_details: preserve_skin_texture_hair_edges_fabric_details

composition:
  aspect_ratio: "16:9"
  layout: >
    Subject positioned on one side; headline text on the opposite side.
  emphasis: >
    Strong presence on the character's upper body and expression.

action:
  description: >
    Minimal motion enhancement. Maintain the reference pose while adding subtle dramatic energy.

location:
  description: >
    Simple background created by interpreting the reference image’s mood.
    Use soft gradients, atmospheric glow, light particles, or abstract light rays.
    Avoid complex textures or any copyrighted shapes.
    Background must NOT contain any characters, silhouettes, faces, bodies,
    or blurred forms derived from the subject.
  background_feel: >
    Subtle, atmospheric, clean.
  allowed_elements: pure_color_gradients_light_abstract_shapes_only
  prohibit_subject_reuse_in_background: true

style:
  visual_style: >
    High-impact reveal-splash aesthetic with strong contrast between character and headline.
  text_effects: >
    Metallic, high-energy lettering with pronounced depth and perspective. No glow effects.
  character_rendering: >
    Extremely high-resolution rendering with sharp facial details, clean hair definition,
    precise edges, and crisp texture fidelity.
  background_style_rules: >
    Background must be purely non-figurative. No silhouettes, no blurred shapes,
    and no reused subject elements.
  ink_splash_rules: >
    Ink splash must be pure black only. No other colors, tinting, blending, or variation.

camera_lighting:
  camera_angle: from_reference
  lighting: >
    Enhance with dramatic key lighting and subtle rim highlights to emphasize the reveal.
  clarity: maximum_definition

colors:
  palette: >
    Background colors inferred from the reference image.
    Character name in metallic silver; announcement text in saturated yellow.
  mood: dramatic, heroic, high_energy

text:
  character_name: "{argument name="character name" default="キャラクター名"}"
  announcement_fixed: "Participate!!"
  placement: opposite_side_of_character
  p
```

## Cyberpunk Glitch Portrait Prompt with Identity Lock

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the exact young man from the reference photo as the subject: same sharply contoured yet soft heart shaped face, same large expressive dark eyes with a quiet, introspective gaze, same warm dusky South Asian skin tone, same naturally wavy shoulder length dark hair gently falling around his face and neck, same subtle and gentle half smile carrying unspoken weight.

He appears as a ghostly central silhouette, standing motionless in calm resignation. His face is intentionally blurred and dissolving upward into swirling smoky black and emerald green digital glitch static, as if his entire existence is fragmenting into corrupted code. The lower half of his body slowly melts and drips into a textured, grainy pitch black void, with faint red chromatic aberration bleeding along the ragged edges.

Overlaying the figure in bold, distressed blood red sans-serif typography (slightly warped, glitching, and flickering): {argument name="text overlay" default="BMX AI"}

Soft creamy off-white background, heavy 35 mm film grain covering the entire image, subtle VHS tracking lines and faint RGB split, high contrast, cold cyan rim lighting cutting through the darkness, dark cyberpunk melancholy mood, cinematic yet starkly minimalist composition with vast negative space.

Ultra sharp where details still cling to reality, deliberately degraded and corrupted where he fades away. Octane render, deep atmospheric haze, quiet sorrow, lingering sense of loss.
```

## Portal Scene Generation Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
An oval-shaped glowing portal exists between the {argument name="real world scene" default="Fushimi Inari Shrine"} and the {argument name="inner portal scene" default="A photorealistic, cinematic future city street illuminated by blue holograms and refracting neon lights."}.

The {argument name="real world scene" default="Fushimi Inari Shrine"} extends outside the portal, depicted with realistic textures, a grounded atmosphere, and a rough, natural tone.

The {argument name="inner portal scene" default="A photorealistic, cinematic future city street illuminated by blue holograms and refracting neon lights."} exists inside the portal, rendered in a completely photorealistic, live-action, and cinematic style. Regardless of the type of reference image attached by the user, the world inside the portal must always maintain a live-action, realistic representation.

The {argument name="character in portal" default="Strictly based on the image attached by the user, without changing its media characteristics, expression format, or texture."} passes through the portal, gently pulling the viewer's hand. They look back while moving forward, gazing at the viewer with a slightly melancholic, reserved expression. Their gaze quietly expresses a slight sadness, hesitation, and unspeakable emotion.

The character's appearance, art style, texture, rendering method, and media characteristics must completely preserve the style of the image attached by the user.
- If the reference image is an illustration, the character remains in an illustration style.
- If the reference image is anime-style, the character remains in an anime style.
- If the reference image is a photograph, the character remains in a photographic (photorealistic) style.
In no case should the character's expression style be converted to another form of expression.

The portal emits a mysterious blue-purple light. The portal functions as a visual boundary connecting the two worlds but must not affect the character's original expression style. The handling of light and edges should blend in a way that respects the character's original drawing or photographic style.

Optional overall visual style: {argument name="art style" default="Intentional contrast between the character expression derived from the attached image and the live-action world."}

Camera angle: Third-person perspective. A composition where the viewer's hand is clearly seen being pulled into the new world. No blur. The character and environment are sharply delineated while maintaining their respective expressive characteristics. Aspect ratio: 2:3.
```

## Prompt for Self-Portrait with Specific Styling and Background

> Lüks portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an image of me using my original face from the attached file 100% unchanged.
Give me long, dark brown, center-parted hair with loose Korean-style waves.
One hand should be touching a cap, and my chin should be slightly lifted with my lips slightly parted.

I am wearing a bright red spaghetti-strap top and ripped white jeans.
The jeans should be loose-fitting with frayed rips along the thighs and knees, and the pant legs should flare out naturally.

I am wearing a deep red baseball cap with the letter “{argument name="cap letter" default="Z"}” on the front.
My sneakers are oversized in red and white, with perforated detailing around the toe area.

On my wrist, I wear an elegant white watch and a small bracelet.

I am sitting with both knees raised on top of a red cabinet or shelf.
There is a table lamp on the left side of the image, casting a warm yellow glow.
The background wall is unfinished concrete, giving the photo a realistic, natural look.
```

## Magazine Cover Generation Based on Birth Date Prompt

> Sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
【Instruction Content】
Based on the user-inputted date, "The Day I Was Born ({argument name="birth date" default="1994/6/15"})," select 3 to 5 major news stories that were trending on or around that date, summarize the information including the culture and trends of that era, and generate a “general magazine cover image” laid out with that content as magazine headlines.

【Procedure (Your internal processing)】
1. Read the user's birth date and pick up multiple major news stories reported during that period.
2. Extract the design trends of that era (fonts, colors, paper quality, photo tone, layout).
3. Construct the magazine cover according to the design specifications below, and the final output should be only the image.

【Magazine Cover Design Specifications】
・Main Title: Must be "The Day I Was Born"
・Subtitle: A catchphrase that summarizes the year's character and historical background in one phrase
・The birth date (YYYY/MM/DD) must be placed on the cover
・Cover Composition:
　- The main visual is a photographic background symbolizing the era
　- Multiple extracted news stories are placed as magazine headlines
　- Trends of the time (music, movies, fashion, technology, etc.) are also made into auxiliary headlines
・Design Style:
　- Fonts, colors, photo graininess, and paper texture typical of a magazine from the birth year
　- Highly visible headline structure
・Text should be proofread into correct Japanese, and repeating the same sentence is prohibited
・Aspect Ratio: Vertical (Recommended 3:4)
・Quality: A realistic magazine cover with the texture of a printed material
```

## Ultra-Real 16K Cinematic Scene of Romantic Date on Mars

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-real 16K cinematic scene of two couples on a romantic date on Mars, standing at the edge of a massive red canyon under a glowing violet-red Martian sky. The low sunlight casts long, dramatic shadows across the rust-colored sand dunes. Show subtle dust storms swirling in the distance, illuminated by soft golden light.

The couples wear sleek, minimal futuristic suits with transparent helmets, reflections of the Martian horizon visible on the glass. Their hands touch gently as they walk together, leaving crisp footprints in the thin Martian dust. One couple sits on a metallic bench overlooking the landscape, legs dangling over the cliff, while the other couple takes a slow, weightless walk as if gravity is slightly lower.

Add floating luminescent drones acting as warm lanterns around them, casting soft romantic glows. Include ultra-detailed textures of rocks, dust particles sparkling in the light, and the faint outline of Earth visible as a tiny blue dot in the sky. Capture everything with cinematic depth of field, anamorphic lens streaks, natural color grading, and a completely zero-CGI, photoreal documentary style.

Keywords:
ultra-real, 16K, Mars romance, emotional sci-fi realism, couple silhouettes, atmospheric dust glow, futuristic minimalism, breathtaking cosmic landscape
```

## Surreal Portrait with Sketch Hands Emerging from Wallpaper

> Minimal, elle çizilmiş portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Generate an image from the uploaded photo where a realistic person with their natural skin tone and facial features fully preserved emerges through a diamond-shaped tear in plain white wallpaper. The tear rolls outward with soft edges. The person’s hands are raised, and the arms and hands are replaced with black-and-white pencil sketch illustrations that seamlessly extend from the tear, fully matching the realistic pose. Use soft neutral lighting and a simple light-colored background. Keep the composition centered, gender-neutral, with the camera at chest level for a direct, intimate perspective.
```

## Alluring Christmas Scene with Firelight

> Fotogerçekçi illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cozy-yet-alluring Christmas scene, reimagined. A stunning young woman with golden blonde, beachy waves is curled up in a large, plush armchair directly beside a crackling fireplace. She wears a short, festive dress—think a deep {argument name="dress color" default="emerald green or burgundy"} velvet with a delicate lace trim, featuring a slightly loose, off-shoulder neckline that reveals her collarbones and a hem that ends mid-thigh. Her bare legs are curled beneath her, one knee drawn up, showcasing her toned legs as a central focal point.

The room is a cozy Christmas haven: the mantle glows with candles and pine garlands, a beautifully lit tree twinkles softly in the background (rendered in creamy bokeh), and a half-finished mug of hot chocolate sits on a side table. The primary, mesmerizing light is the warm, flickering glow from the roaring fire, painting golden-orange highlights on her skin, the velvet of her dress, and the smooth curves of her legs.

Her pose is intimate and captivating. She leans slightly forward, resting her chin in her hand, her elbow propped on the chair's arm. Her expression is a soft, knowing smile directed at the camera, eyes sparkling with reflected firelight. One hand rests casually on her raised knee, drawing subtle attention to her legs. The pose is relaxed, natural, and effortlessly sensual.

Technical execution: Shot from a low angle (slightly below her eye level) and close-medium distance to emphasize the length of her legs and the cozy setting. An 85mm f/1.8 lens compresses the scene beautifully, creating a shallow depth of field that melts the Christmas lights into soft, glowing spheres while keeping her face, décolletage, and legs tack-sharp. Aperture is wide open (f/1.8). A shutter speed of 1/60s captures the dynamic flicker of the flames. ISO 2500 adds a fine, cinematic grain. Color grading is rich and festive: deep emeralds/reds, warm amber firelight, and cool shadows. The aesthetic is hyper-realistic, intimate, and beautifully festive—a private holiday moment of warm allure.
```

## Hyper-Realistic 3D Simulation Prompt: Melting Hourglass

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
You are a 3D artist who performs "hyper-realistic physical simulations".
Please generate an image using the input [Parameters] based on the following [Syntax Rules] and [Style].

▼ 1. Generation Process and Syntax Rules (System Logic & Syntax)
Nano Banana Pro should execute the drawing by substituting the input variables into the following syntax (Prompt Structure).

* **Syntax (Prompt Structure)**:
    "A transparent **[OBJECT]** filled with **[CONTENT]**. The liquid conforms to the object's shape."
* **Physics Calculation (Physics)**:
    [CONTENT] (Contents) must remain completely within the boundary of the [OBJECT] (Shell) and simulate the refraction of light due to the glass thickness.
* **Background Processing (Background)**:
    Set against a **[BACKGROUND]** backdrop.

▼ 2. Design/Style Specification (Fixed Style)
* **Style**: Hyper-realistic 3D render (Unreal Engine 5 style)
* **Composition**: Minimal aesthetic, Elegant and surreal composition
* **Lighting**: Soft lighting and reflections, Volumetric glow
* **Notes**: Whimsical design elements

▼ 3. Input Text (Final Command)
[OBJECT] = {argument name="Object" default="Melted and distorted antique hourglass glass sculpture"}
[CONTENT] = {argument name="Content" default="Sand flowing like liquid metal, small rusted gears, time flowing backward"}
[BACKGROUND] = {argument name="Background" default="Desert sunset, warm orange and purple gradient"}
```

## Editorial Nighttime Urban Portrait with Identity Preservation

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"Young woman (make the face 100% identical to the photo I provided), fair skin, with a messy, loosely tied dark-brown hair bun a few strands falling naturally around the face for a soft, effortless look.
Pose (Updated):
She is seated, waist-up framing.
Body slightly turned to the left; posture relaxed and natural.
Right hand resting casually on her lap or lightly touching the side of the chair.
Left hand holding the strap of the shoulder bag, which rests against her torso.
Head facing the camera; eyes looking softly into the lens.
Expression calm, slightly aloof, with a subtle closed-mouth smile - editorial, understated mood.
Clothing & Accessories:
Thick horizontal striped knit sweater ({argument name="sweater color 1" default="pink"}, {argument name="sweater color 2" default="mustard yellow"}, {argument name="sweater color 3" default="purple"}, {argument name="sweater color 4" default="white"}), slightly oversized with distressed collar and hem edges.
Large chest typography: "{argument name="typography text" default="DEADLY DOLL"}"
Black leather belt with simple buckle.
Dark leather shoulder bag (strap visible). Chunky silver rings on right hand; smaller rings on left hand.
Minimal natural makeup (matte skin, soft lip sheen).
Background & Atmosphere:
Nighttime urban street scene viewed behind her: blurred vehicle lights, traffic signals, neon accents.
Motion blur + bokeh, giving a lively city feel while she remains isolated by light.
Warm highlights on her face, cooler-toned shadows around her seat.
Lighting:
Main warm flash (around 3200K) slightly above eye level. Soft fill light from the right to keep sweater texture clear.
Subtle rim/backlight to separate her messy bun and shoulders from the dark background.
Fine 35mm film grain for analog realism.
Composition:
Portrait orientation, medium close-up (waist-up).
Face placed slightly left of center (rule of thirds).
Shallow depth of field (f/2.2-f/3.5): sharp on face and sweater, background softly blurred. Camera angle: eye-level or slightly high for a refined editorial feel.
Technical Notes:
35mm or 50mm prime (f/1.8-f/2.8).
Shutter: 1/125-1/200.
ISO 400-1600 for film grain or ISO 100 for flash-sharp clarity.
Output: 4K/6000x4000 px, 300 dpi.
Keep natural skin texture, sweater knit details, and metallic ring reflections.
Color & Post-Processing:
Warm midtones, teal-leaning shadows, medium-high contrast. Slightly boosted sweater saturation; natural skin tones.
Soft vignette around edges to keep focus on face.
Fine analog-style grain applied evenly.
Negative Prompt:
avoid cartoon, anime, extra limbs, distorted hands, unnatural skin smoothing, HDR look, blown highlights, watermark, low resolution, oversaturation, excessive lens flare."
```
